- **security-expert**: &gt; **Do not rename this file to `security.md`.** OpenSSF Scorecard finds the &gt; repository's security policy by filename, and its path matcher falls back to &gt; comparing the **basename** — so any `security.md` anywhere in the tree is a &gt; candidate. - **security-review**: Independent security-contract lane for the baton. On a sensitive seam, its methodology is to derive the seam's authorization/tenant/egress invariants from source and resolve the seam's configured/default (deployed) behavior, then judge the seam against them — checking what the seam does in production, not only the changed lines. Read-only, its own context — it is the independent specialist the zero-trust verify step consults.
